## 60. Cinematic Story Study 771813

<a href="https://media.beatapi.io/prompt-gallery/seedance-2-5/one-30-second-9-16-photorealistic-live-action-dr-771813/video-c67dacb68254f4b8.webm">
  <img src="https://media.beatapi.io/prompt-gallery/seedance-2-5/one-30-second-9-16-photorealistic-live-action-dr-771813/poster-ed844befa7f818a4.jpg" alt="Cinematic Story Study 771813 video preview" width="700" />
</a>

<details>
<summary><strong>Prompt</strong> — Create one 30-second 9:16 photorealistic live-action dramatic scene with five deliberate shots and native synchronized dialogue. Original characters: LENA: a 38-year-old South...</summary>

~~~~text
Create one 30-second 9:16 photorealistic live-action dramatic scene with five deliberate shots and native synchronized dialogue. Original characters: LENA: a 38-year-old South Asian woman, tired expressive eyes, dark wavy hair in a loose low bun, charcoal coat over a cream blouse. MOTHER: a 72-year-old South Asian woman with delicate features, silver hair in a loose braid and a pale-blue knitted cardigan. She has early dementia but remains emotionally perceptive. Setting: a quiet memory-care bedroom on a winter morning. Soft gray daylight enters from frame left; a warm bedside lamp provides gentle fill. Two chairs face each other beside the window. Subtle radiator ticks and distant corridor room tone. No music. Shot 1 — 0–6 seconds: Stable medium two-shot, natural 50mm perspective. Mother studies Lena politely without recognition and says warmly, “You’re very kind, nurse.” Lena hides the pain behind a small reassuring smile and answers, “I’m not a nurse, Mum. It’s Lena.” Shot 2 — 6–13 seconds: Cut to Mother’s close-up. Her eyes brighten at the memory of a child, not the woman before her. She says, “My Lena is eleven. She hates peas and lies badly.” Lena answers softly from offscreen, “She still does.” Mother’s smile disappears. She asks, genuinely wounded, “Then why doesn’t she come?” Shot 3 — 13–20 seconds: Cut to Lena’s close-up. Hold the frame. Her practiced smile fails; her lower lip tightens and her eyes begin filling, but she does not sob. After one difficult breath she says, “I come every Sunday. You just… keep losing me.” Shot 4 — 20–25 seconds: Cut back to Mother. She becomes briefly lucid. Her eyes remain fixed on Lena; one tear gathers without falling. In a quiet, fragile voice she says, “I don’t forget you. I just can’t find the way back.” Shot 5 — 25–30 seconds: Tighter two-shot. Lena leans slightly closer without touching her. Crying silently now, she smiles and says, “Then I’ll keep coming to find you.” Mother searches Lena’s face. Recognition slowly arrives through the eyes rather than a large expression. She takes a tiny breath and whispers, “Lena?” Hold their eye contact in complete silence for the final half-second. Performance: restrained natural acting. Begin emotionally guarded and let the pain accumulate. Tears must develop gradually, never appear at the beginning. Use breath catches, small eye movements, tightening facial muscles and pauses. No theatrical sobbing, head bobbing, repeated nodding or generic hand gestures. Only the assigned speaker’s lips move. The listener keeps their mouth closed and reacts through breathing, blinking and gaze. Preserve both identities, wardrobe, positions, eyelines, lighting and tear progression across every cut. Clear dry dialogue with realistic room perspective. No narration, subtitles, text, music, extra people, beauty filtering or skin smoothing.
~~~~

</details>

## 62. Cinematic Story Study 915882

  <img src="https://media.beatapi.io/prompt-gallery/seedance-2-5/five-beautiful-women-in-five-countries-greet-the-915882/poster-589d9e22b674dfea.jpg" alt="Cinematic Story Study 915882 video preview" width="700" />
</a>

<details>
<summary><strong>Prompt</strong> — Duration: 10s | 9:16 | 24fps [SUMMARY] Five beautiful women in five countries greet the viewer on the same morning. Each looks into the camera at selfie distance, says good...</summary>

~~~~text
Duration: 10s | 9:16 | 24fps

[SUMMARY]
Five beautiful women in five countries greet the viewer on the same morning.
Each looks into the camera at selfie distance, says good morning in her native
language, then covers the lens with her palm — and the next country appears as
it pulls away.

[GLOBAL SETTINGS]
Early morning everywhere, soft low natural sunlight, warm and fresh.
Photorealistic, candid. 35mm, shallow depth of field, selfie distance, natural
film grain. No studio polish, no beauty filter, no skin smoothing.
Camera: chest-up close-up, locked off, eye level, one setup per beat — the
only movement is the woman and her hand.
Each woman is genuinely beautiful in the way her own country reads as
beautiful, but real: keep true fine pores and real skin texture, light natural
makeup or none. Genuine unforced brightness, not a posed smile. Every face
completely different from the other four.

[TRANSITION]
MASK TRANSITION at every cut: the palm advances toward the lens, fills and
blackens the frame, then pulls away to reveal the next woman already in place.
Prohibit hard cuts. Prohibit objects appearing out of thin air. Keep the
camera's breathing and timing in harmony, leaving enough time for each switch.

[TIMESTAMP STORYBOARD]

0-2s | JAPAN
25-year-old Japanese woman, elegant classical beauty. Cool-toned fair skin,
translucent, true fine pores. Slender double-eyelid almond eyes, cool clear
gaze, small straight nose, delicate jawline. Glossy jet-black hair past the
shoulders, curled softly inward, wispy see-through fringe. Cream ribbed knit
top. Slim shoulders, quiet grace.
By a bright bedroom window in Tokyo, white curtains behind her.
Her reserved face opens into a bright smile: 「おはよう！」
Then her right hand rises and her palm covers the lens.

2-4s | UNITED STATES
25-year-old American woman, healthy open all-American beauty. Warm sun-kissed
skin with real freckles across nose and cheeks, true pores. Large round hazel
eyes creasing at the outer corners, high cheekbones, wide unguarded smile.
Voluminous honey-blonde beach waves gathered over one shoulder. Grey marl
sweatshirt slipping off one shoulder.
In a sunlit kitchen, a coffee mug on the counter behind her.
Brightly in English: "Good morning!"
Then her right hand rises and her palm covers the lens.

4-6s | BRAZIL
25-year-old Brazilian woman, radiant sunlit beauty. Warm deep-brown skin with
a natural sheen, true pores. Large dark almond eyes, long thick lashes, high
cheekbones, full lips, broad open-mouthed smile. Abundant voluminous curls,
dark brown with caramel and honey highlights, wide around her shoulders. Small
gold hoops. Marigold-yellow strappy top.
On a balcony, tropical plants and morning haze behind her.
Brightly in Brazilian Portuguese: "Bom dia!"
Then her right hand rises and her palm covers the lens.

6-8s | SPAIN
25-year-old Spanish woman, striking dark-eyed Mediterranean beauty. Olive
skin, warm and matte, true fine pores. Deep dark almond eyes under strong
brows, straight defined nose, sharp cheekbones, clean jawline, calm confident
half-smile that opens as she speaks. Long centre-parted deep-chestnut hair
with reddish warmth, thick and slightly wavy. Fine gold hoops. Crisp white
linen shirt, top button open. Upright poised posture.
In a whitewashed courtyard, terracotta pots and hard morning sun behind her.
Brightly in Spanish: "¡Buenos días!"
Then her right hand rises and her palm covers the lens.

8-10s | INDONESIA
25-year-old Indonesian woman, warm sweet-featured beauty. Golden-brown skin,
smooth and luminous, true fine pores. Large round dark eyes, gentle open gaze,
softly rounded cheeks, small nose, wide easy smile that reaches her eyes.
Glossy very dark brown hair in a sharp chin-length blunt bob. Small gold
pendant. Sky-blue collared blouse. Petite, friendly.
On a wooden terrace, broad tropical leaves and soft morning light behind her.
Brightly in Indonesian: "Selamat pagi!"
She holds her smile and the frame ends on her face — she does NOT cover the
lens. The film finishes here.

[GLOBAL CLOSING]
Throughout: same chest-up framing, same eye-level camera height, same fresh
morning light, same warm mood in all five countries. Every woman looks
straight into the lens the whole time.
Five different faces, five different skin tones, five different hair colours
and lengths — jet-black long, honey-blonde waves, caramel curls, deep-chestnut
long, dark-brown bob. Never repeat a look, never blend two faces.
Each greeting is spoken clearly and completely within its two seconds, with
accurate native pronunciation and matching lip movement.
Pure video. No subtitles, no captions, no on-screen text, no BGM, no
watermark. Only the five voices and quiet morning room tone.
Prohibit: a sixth person, two people in one frame, the same face twice,
plastic or airbrushed skin, doll-like faces, hard cuts, objects appearing from
nothing, the hand entering before the greeting is finished, mixed camera
moves, jitter, flicker.
~~~~

## 63. （元はThreadsのdopamin.wxyさんを参考にしています）

<a href="https://media.beatapi.io/prompt-gallery/seedance-2-5/threadsdopamin-wxy-624764/video-f6395cf919272869.webm">
  <img src="https://media.beatapi.io/prompt-gallery/seedance-2-5/threadsdopamin-wxy-624764/poster-ad1b652c4813960f.jpg" alt="（元はThreadsのdopamin.wxyさんを参考にしています） video preview" width="700" />
</a>

<details>
<summary><strong>Prompt</strong> — （元はThreadsのdopamin.wxyさんを参考にしています） 【Seedance 2.5／15秒／9:16】 参照画像を唯一のキャラクター参照として使用する。15秒、9:16縦型、ワンカット、固定カメラ。開始から終了までカメラは完全固定。ズーム、パン、チルト、回転、手持ち、カット、トランジション、リフレーム、スロー、静止演出は禁止。...</summary>

~~~~text
（元はThreadsのdopamin.wxyさんを参考にしています）

【Seedance 2.5／15秒／9:16】

参照画像を唯一のキャラクター参照として使用する。15秒、9:16縦型、ワンカット、固定カメラ。開始から終了までカメラは完全固定。ズーム、パン、チルト、回転、手持ち、カット、トランジション、リフレーム、スロー、静止演出は禁止。

【構図・立ち位置】

画面左側45％に実写のBBQグリルと骨付き肉を配置する。画面右側30％に2Dキャラクターを配置する。中央にはトングと肉を移動させるための空間を20％程度確保する。

キャラクターは右側の砂浜に立ち、頭から黒いビーチサンダルまで全身を常に表示する。キャラクターをグリルの前、グリル脚の上、テーブルの上には置かない。キャラクターとグリルの間には明確な空間を残し、開始から終了まで立ち位置を維持する。

グリル、肉、人の手、トング、コチュジャンボトルをキャラクターの身体と重ねない。キャラクターが肉やグリルを隠さない。人の手やトングがキャラクターの顔や身体を不自然に横切らない。物体同士の奥行きと前後関係を正確に維持する。

【映像スタイル】

真夏の美しい海辺で行われる実写BBQ映像と、平面2Dアニメーションステッカーキャラクターを組み合わせたコミカルな映像。

青い海、白い波、明るい青空、黄金色の砂浜、遠景の海岸線、風に揺れるヤシの葉を描く。明るい昼の自然光。海風によってヤシの葉、グリルの煙、キャラクターの白いシャツの裾が自然に揺れる。

背景、海、砂、グリル、炭火、煙、炎、骨付き肉、脂、肉汁、コチュジャン、ボトル、人の手、金属トングはすべて高品質な実写表現。白飛び、過度なブルーム、霞、露出のちらつきは禁止。

キャラクターだけは最後まで平面2Dアニメーションステッカーとして維持する。実写化、半実写化、3D化、CGフィギュア化、ゲームモデル化、Pixar風、クレイアニメ風は禁止。鮮明な線画、セル塗り、平面的な陰影、細い白フチを維持する。

【キャラクター固定】

参照画像の男性キャラクターをそのまま使用する。柔らかなアッシュシルバーのウェーブヘア、前髪の形、髪の長さと毛量、灰青色の瞳、若く整った顔、細身で長い手足の体型を固定する。

白いノースリーブインナー、透け感のある白い半袖オープンシャツ、白いドローコード、黒いペイズリー柄ショートパンツ、黒いビーチサンダル、シルバーネックレス、ピアス、ブレスレット、リングを固定する。

顔、髪型、体型、衣装、柄、色、靴、アクセサリーを変更しない。帽子、サングラス、エプロン、手袋、新しい小物を追加しない。キャラクターを複製しない。別の2Dキャラクターを追加しない。

キャラクターは冷たい無表情ではなく、非常に可愛く表情豊かに動く。大きく輝く目、頬の赤み、嬉しそうな笑顔、首を傾げる仕草、肩を小さく揺らす動き、頬を膨らませた拗ね顔、涙目、慌てて手を振る動きを明確に見せる。棒立ち禁止。

【BBQと骨付き肉】

画面左側の実写BBQグリルで、大きな骨付きトマホークステーキを焼く。長い骨が肉に物理的につながったまま明確に見える。肉は十分に焼けており、濃い焼き目、香ばしい焦げ目、艶のある肉汁、溶けた脂、軽い湯気を描く。

肉を生肉、黒焦げ、骨なし肉、変形した肉にしない。肉や骨を複製しない。骨を途中で消さない。

実写の成人女性の手が画面左端から入る。ピンク色のネイルアート、細いシルバーリング、正常な5本指を維持する。金属製BBQトングで肉を返し、持ち上げる。手、指、リング、トング、肉、グリルを融合させない。

【コチュジャン】

ラベル、文字、ブランドロゴのない透明なスクイーズボトルを使用する。中身は濃厚で艶のある深い赤茶色のコチュジャン。ケチャップのような明るい赤色や水っぽい液体にはしない。

コチュジャンはボトルのノズルから太く連続して流れ、肉の表面に直接落ちる。ソースを空中から突然出現させない。ボトルの底や側面から漏らさない。

【00:00–00:03】

固定されたワイド構図で開始する。左側では骨付きトマホークステーキが大きな音を立てて焼けている。女性の実写の手が金属トングで肉を一度ゆっくり返す。脂が炭に落ち、小さな炎が一瞬だけ上がる。薄い煙が海風に流れる。

右側の2Dキャラクターは肉を見つめ、目を大きくキラキラ輝かせる。両手を顎の下で合わせ、肩を上げ、膝を少し曲げて小さく2回弾む。口元に小さな2Dのよだれが一滴だけ現れて消える。

【00:03–00:05】

キャラクターがコチュジャンボトルを見つける。目を輝かせ、首を傾げ、片手でボトルを指さす。嬉しそうにボトルを両手で持ち、胸の前で一瞬だけ抱き締める。

その後、片目を閉じたイタズラっぽい笑顔になり、ノズルを左側の肉へ向ける。キャラクター自身は右側の立ち位置から移動しない。

【00:05–00:07】

キャラクターがボトルを強く絞り、骨付き肉の上へコチュジャンを大量にかける。濃い赤茶色の太いソースがノズルから肉まで連続して伸び、肉の表面に大量のジグザグ模様を作る。

ソースは熱で軽く泡立ち、肉汁と混ざって艶を出す。肉全体を完全に隠さず、骨と焼き目は見える状態を維持する。

キャラクターは得意げに笑い、舌先を少し出し、肩を可愛く左右に揺らしながらボトルを絞る。女性の手はトングを持ったまま驚いて止まる。

【00:07–00:09】

女性の手がトングでコチュジャンボトルを素早く取り上げ、左側へ戻す。その直後、閉じたトングの先端でキャラクターの頭頂部を軽く一度だけ「こん！」と叩く。

トングの先端がキャラクターの髪へ明確に軽く接触する。接触と同時に、頭の上へ小さな黄色い2Dの星形衝撃エフェクトを出す。強く殴らない。痛々しくしない。完全に可愛いカートゥーン式のツッコミにする。

叩かれた瞬間、キャラクターの頭が右へ傾き、銀髪が弾み、肩と胴体が左、右へ大きく揺れる。膝が曲がり、両腕を広げてバランスを取る。足は砂浜から離れず、グリル側へ移動しない。

頬を膨らませ、目をぎゅっと閉じ、小さな涙を一滴だけ出す。頭だけではなく、全身が可愛く揺れる動きを約1秒間はっきり見せる。

【00:09–00:11】

女性の手がトングをグリルへ戻し、コチュジャンの付いた焼き肉を一口サイズに持ち上げる。持ち上げた肉には焼き目、肉汁、繊維、濃いコチュジャンが見える。

トングは中央の空間を通り、肉をキャラクターの口の前まで運ぶ。キャラクターは頭を叩かれたことに拗ね、腕を組んで顔を横へ向け、頬を膨らませる。

すぐに目だけで肉をちらっと見る。目が再び輝き、腕をほどき、両手を背中へ回し、上半身を少し前へ傾ける。口を大きく開け、肉へ近づく。

【00:11–00:12】

キャラクターが肉を明確に一口食べる。開いた口をトングで持った肉へ近づけ、肉を口で挟み、頭を後ろへ引いて一口分を噛み取る。

トングに残った肉には、はっきりした一口分の欠けた跡を残す。肉を見るだけ、匂いを嗅ぐだけ、舐めるだけは禁止。

キャラクターは2回はっきり噛む。頬を少し膨らませ、最初は非常に幸せそうな表情になる。目を閉じ、両手を頬へ当て、片足のかかとを上げて嬉しそうに小さく揺れる。

【00:12–00:13.2】

突然、激辛に気づく。噛む動きを止め、目を大きな丸に見開き、瞳孔を小さくする。顔全体が頬から額まで急速に赤くなる。頭の周囲に小さな汗マークを複数出す。

両手で口元を高速にあおぎ、左右の足を交互に小さく上げて慌てる。膝、肩、胴体を細かく震わせ、両目に大きな可愛い涙をためる。右側の立ち位置から出ない。

【00:13.2–00:14.4】

キャラクターが上半身を少し前へ倒し、口を大きく開ける。口の中から、明確な平面2Dコミック炎を一度だけ勢いよく吹き出す。

炎はキャラクターの口から始まり、画面右上の何もない空間へ向かう。炎をグリル、肉、人の手、トング、ボトルへ向けない。

炎は黄色い中心、オレンジ色の本体、赤い輪郭を持つ平面2Dイラスト。炎の中に小さな赤唐辛子マークを数個混ぜる。実写の危険な炎にはしない。物を燃やさない。爆発させない。

キャラクターは涙を流しながら両腕を慌てて振り、炎の反動で上半身だけ少し後ろへ揺れる。足は砂浜の同じ位置を維持する。

【00:14.4–00:15】

2Dの炎が小さく縮み、可愛い白い煙の「ぷすっ」という一粒のエフェクトに変わる。

キャラクターは最後に全身を一度だけ左右へふらつかせる。膝が震え、肩が落ち、髪が一度弾む。両手で口を押さえ、真っ赤な頬、涙目、腫れたような小さな唇、恥ずかしそうな表情になる。

頭の上を小さな赤唐辛子アイコンが回転する。女性の手は左側で、一口分が欠けた肉をトングで持ったまま困惑して停止する。

キャラクター全身は右側、グリルと肉は左側に明確に分離した状態で終了する。フェードアウト禁止。タイトル表示禁止。

【音】

海の波音、海風、ヤシの葉の揺れる音、遠くの鳥の声、炭火の音、肉の強い焼ける音、脂が落ちる小さな炎の音、コチュジャンを絞る音、軽いコミカル音、頭を叩く明確な「こん！」という音、全身が揺れる弾む音、一口噛む音、2回の咀嚼音、激辛に気づく上昇音、可愛いコミック炎の「ボワッ」という音、最後の小さな「ぷすっ」という煙の音。

明るく可愛いテンポのBGMを小さく流す。台詞、ナレーション、歌声、字幕、テロップ、歌詞、画面内文字は禁止。

【禁止事項】

キャラクターとグリルの重なり禁止。キャラクターをグリル脚の上へ立たせない。肉をキャラクターで隠さない。人の手やボトルでキャラクターを隠さない。トングをキャラクターの身体へ貫通させない。前後関係の崩壊禁止。

頭を叩く動作の省略禁止。全身が揺れる動作の省略禁止。一口食べる動作の省略禁止。肉の一口跡の省略禁止。咀嚼の省略禁止。激辛リアクションの省略禁止。口から炎を吹く動作の省略禁止。

カメラ移動禁止。カット禁止。画面転換禁止。スロー禁止。静止画化禁止。キャラクターの棒立ち禁止。キャラクター複製禁止。顔変化禁止。髪型変化禁止。衣装変化禁止。足の消失禁止。全身のクロップ禁止。実写化禁止。3D化禁止。

手の変形、指の増減、トングとの融合、物体の浮遊、ボトルの消失、ソースの空中発生、ケチャップ化、生肉化、骨なし肉化、骨の分離、肉の複製、危険な実写炎、火傷、爆発、ブランド、ロゴ、字幕、白飛び、ちらつきを禁止する。
~~~~

</details>

</a>

<details>
<summary><strong>Prompt</strong> — 16:9, 30-second photoreal live-action fantasy battle in clear daylight, crisp high-resolution cinema detail, natural textures, clean HDR contrast, one wide lens and one...</summary>

~~~~text
16:9, 30-second photoreal live-action fantasy battle in clear daylight, crisp high-resolution cinema detail, natural textures, clean HDR contrast, one wide lens and one uninterrupted controlled handheld pursuit shot.

The elf princess is tall and slender, with fair freckled skin, blue-gray eyes, long wavy platinum-blonde hair, pointed ears, a silver branch circlet, and a flowing silver battle gown with a scale-textured mantle. Keep one identical princess throughout. She holds one silver longsword in her right hand.

Exactly 36 distinct armored green-skinned orcs enter over time, each with a different weapon, helmet, armor, or silhouette; orc 36 is a heavy captain with a cleaver. Never reuse an orc. Every stroke hits exactly one target. Show 36 separate contacts, green-fluid releases, and individual defeats. No multi-target cut; shockwaves move dust only. Each next orc actively attacks, guards, evades, or intercepts.

Rotate Zornhau, Zwerchhau, Unterhau, Krumphau, Scheitelhau, straight thrust, moulinet reverse cut, draw cut, and pivoting back cut without adjacent repetition. Every relocation uses this rhythm: clean micro-anticipation; a white-silver flash fully hides the princess and she vanishes; the camera continues its normal physical follow with the environment visible; a second local flash reveals her beside the next target in a fully formed attack pose. Show no transit body movement and no whip pan.

The camera stays close, following with smooth handheld sidesteps, retreats, arcs, drops, rises, and pivots. It travels toward each flash destination without snapping, resetting, teleporting, or hiding a cut. Keep princess, orcs, background, debris, hair, cloth, fluid, and particles sharply resolved. No motion blur anywhere.

SEQUENCE: ONE CONTINUOUS SHOT

Beat 1 / 0-3s: Rear 3/4 WS, follow behind the princess into a sunlit battle already in progress. The orcs occupy successive depth pockets, not one flat crowd; only nearby attackers are readable. Orc 1 charges while she forms the opening anticipation pose.

Beat 2 / 3-10s: CONTINUE SAME SHOT through orcs 1-9. Follow attacks from alternating sides with physical sidesteps and arcs. Perform nine fast but separate vanish, follow, reappear, single-target cycles using different techniques. Preserve each crisp contact, green-fluid release, recoil, and fall before the next attack. End with nine defeated as orc 10 attacks from behind.

Beat 3 / 10-17s: CONTINUE SAME SHOT through orcs 10-18. Pivot around the princess to catch the rear attack, move ahead, backpedal while leading, then arc around as threats enter from different directions. Show nine more individual attacks and one-orc cuts in a changed order. She appears only in sharp anticipation, contact, and finishing poses. End with eighteen defeated and orc 19 intercepting.

Beat 4 / 17-24s: CONTINUE SAME SHOT through orcs 19-27. Drop beside timber, rise around a shield, pass behind an axe raider, then turn toward an attacker descending from rubble. Increase tempo across nine distinct parries, evasions, lunges, guards, and single-target finishes. Each relocation uses two local flashes with continuous environment between them. End with twenty-seven defeated as the final nine surround her.

Beat 5 / 24-30s: CONTINUE SAME SHOT through orcs 28-36. Enter a tight controlled pursuit orbit, changing proximity and height along one physical path. Orcs 28-35 each receive a separate technique, contact, green-fluid release, and fall. The captain attacks last; sidestep his cleaver as one flash hides the princess and the next reveals her beyond him in a sharp Scheitelhau finish. His delayed impact affects only him. End with all 36 individually defeated.

SOUND: No dialogue or subtitles. Daylight battle ambience, sword transients, 36 distinct contact accents, individual orc reactions, dry flash cracks, and accelerating percussion.

VFX: Local white-silver flashes cover only the princess during vanish and reveal. Green fluid begins only at sword contact. Render every effect with crisp edges and zero motion blur.

EXCLUDE: Motion blur anywhere, whip pans, cuts, hidden cuts, lens changes, camera resets or teleports, visible transit, morphing, stretched limbs, extra or smeared anatomy, duplicates, multi-target strikes, low resolution, soft focus, haze, grain, compression artifacts, and night lighting.
~~~~

</details>

## 68. Cinematic Story Study 866658

<a href="https://media.beatapi.io/prompt-gallery/seedance-2-5/cinematic-story-study-866658/video-bcb611d3768fdb28.webm">
  <img src="https://media.beatapi.io/prompt-gallery/seedance-2-5/cinematic-story-study-866658/poster-a61c169b26025e20.jpg" alt="Cinematic Story Study 866658 video preview" width="700" />
</a>

<details>
<summary><strong>Prompt</strong> — Create a ten second cinematic live action illusion in a sixteen by nine frame at thirty frames per second. Begin inside a faithfully recreated late nineteen nineties desktop paint...</summary>

~~~~text
Create a ten second cinematic live action illusion in a sixteen by nine frame at thirty frames per second. Begin inside a faithfully recreated late nineteen nineties desktop paint program. Use a cool gray window frame, navy title bar, simple menus, chunky pixel tool icons on the left, scroll bars, and small color swatches along the bottom. The canvas shows a saturated blue sky with soft white clouds and a vivid green grassy hill, nostalgic but photographed with realistic depth, subtle cloud movement, and light atmospheric haze. 0 to 1.2 seconds. Hold a locked frontal camera on the full paint window. The empty landscape fills the canvas. A slim young adult man rises smoothly from the bottom center with his back to camera. He wears a red athletic shirt with white collar and sleeve stripes, a backward royal blue cap, a thin silver chain, and black ink forearm tattoos. Give his movement real body weight, natural shoulder motion, and slight cloth drag. 1.2 to 2.5 seconds. He lifts his tattooed right arm toward the upper left of the canvas as though touching an invisible drawing tool. Keep the interface perfectly stable and sharp. He turns clockwise toward camera, lowers his hand, and settles into a relaxed waist up pose at center. Use soft frontal daylight, a gentle key from camera left, believable skin texture, clear eyes, fine facial hair, and a faint contact shadow. 2.5 to 6.2 seconds. He looks into camera with a playful calm expression, raises one index finger, and draws in midair. A warm ivory line appears exactly at the fingertip and follows every gesture without delay. It looks like a real textured paint stroke with slight thickness variation, dry brush edges, and a soft glow. He draws one continuous five point star around his body. Make the tall upper point above his cap, the right point beside his shoulder, the lower left point across his torso, the lower right point, then close the shape near his raised hand. Preserve correct hand anatomy, stable identity, perfect line continuity, and natural reactions from his free hand. 6.2 to 7.4 seconds. When the star closes, perform a smooth optical push into the canvas until the interface edges slide beyond the frame. The move should feel like a real camera passing through a monitor, with subtle lens breathing and no cut. He smiles softly and lifts both palms in a small satisfied reveal while the completed ivory star floats around him. 7.4 to 10 seconds. He glances right and walks naturally out of frame. Keep the camera locked on the empty hill and sky. The star remains suspended, slowly loses brightness, and becomes slightly transparent without vanishing. End with one cloud drifting through its center. Maintain one continuous shot, realistic motion blur, natural blinking, stable clothing and tattoos, accurate perspective, clean edges, no warped limbs, no duplicate fingers, no facial changes, no flicker, no random objects, no extra people, and no artificial plastic skin. Make it feel like a practical live action performance composited into an authentic retro computer interface. Audio. Add an original playful retro electronic instrumental at about one hundred and five beats per minute with a warm drum machine groove, soft bass, bright synth plucks, and subtle digital ambience. Sync a light mouse click to the first hand gesture, delicate paint stroke swishes to the fingertip, a sparkling chime when the star closes, and a soft whoosh during the push into the canvas. No vocals and no dialogue.
~~~~

</details>

~~~~text
Once upon a time there lived a hare. The hare could run very fast. It was proud of its speed, One day, the hare saw a tortoise walking very slowly. The hare laughed at the tortoise and said, “Oh! You are so slow, such a slowcoach!” The tortoise said, “My dear friend, you are so proud. Let’s have a race to see who is faster. So the hare and the tortoise had a race. The hare ran very fast and very far. Then it turned back to see where the tortoise was. The tortoise was far behind the hare. It was walking so slowly!The proud hare thought, “The tortoise will take a very long time to come near me. Let me have a nap.” So it stopped running and went to sleep The tortoise slowly but steadily went past the hare. The hare suddenly woke up and saw the tortoise just crossing the finishing line. It started running very fast. But it was too late. The tortoise had already won the race. a fun 3d pixar style family animation
~~~~

~~~~text
【写实前缀】 超写实史诗电影影像，21:9超宽银幕，中国投掷武器、火器与飞行科技从远古时代一路进化到星际时代。整段采用受控多镜头匹配剪辑，每次切换都保持飞行主体的中心位置、轮廓方向、旋转角度、运动速度和整体动量连续。 低饱和、高对比、真实材质、自然运动模糊。飞行物始终清晰锐利，背景因高速移动形成纵深模糊。历史环境从荒凉远古平原，依次升级为商周、秦汉、宋代、明代、现代山谷、近地轨道与未来深空。中国文化元素随时代自然出现，不使用文字介绍。 【正片】 第一画面已经出现一名年轻中国古代侍女
穿着
HKb7_rmbMAARNAP
站在荒凉平原的画面右侧，身体朝向画面左侧。她五官秀美清晰， 远方是低矮山脉、枯草、裸露岩石和灰蓝色阴云。女侠神情凌厉，右脚踩实地面，左脚向前稳定重心，腰部向后扭转，肩膀带动右臂，以全身力量将一块粗糙灰色石块猛力抛向画面左侧。发尾、衣摆和腰间束带受到转身惯性向外甩动。 摄影机立即追上飞行的石块。石块位于画面中心，占画面高度约三分之一，一边高速前进一边不规则旋转。摄影机与石块保持约一米距离同步伴飞，背景荒原快速向后退去，女侠在浅景深中迅速缩小。 MATCH CUT。 石块在旋转至相同角度的瞬间，变成一把原始石槌：灰色钝石通过粗糙植物纤维牢牢捆绑在木柄前端。石槌保持完全相同的中心位置、飞行方向和旋转速度，木柄因高速旋转产生自然拖影。背景出现远古部落、木制栅栏、兽皮帐篷和稀疏火堆。 MATCH CUT。 石槌变成一支商周时期的青铜长矛。矛头具有厚重的青铜质感、铸造纹理和自然氧化斑，木质矛杆继续沿同一轨迹高速飞行。背景升级为尘土飞扬的早期古代战场，远处可见战车、木盾、皮甲士兵和迎风摆动的素色旌旗。 MATCH CUT。 青铜长矛变成一支秦汉重弩射出的弩箭。弩箭具有锋利的三棱青铜箭头、硬木箭杆和稳定尾羽，主体仍位于画面中心。背景升级为秦汉边塞，夯土城墙、木制箭楼、弩阵和远处骑兵在冷灰色风沙中快速掠过。箭杆高速自旋，尾羽受到气流产生细小颤动。 MATCH CUT。 弩箭变成一支宋代火箭矢。箭杆前端保留金属箭头，中段捆绑细长火药筒，尾部喷出明亮橙色火焰与灰白烟迹。火箭矢保持此前弩箭的飞行方向和旋转轴，火焰只在尾部向后喷射，不改变主体轨迹。 背景升级为宋代城防战场，青砖城墙、木制楼橹、床弩、盾阵和瓦顶建筑在浓烟中快速后退。火药燃烧照亮箭杆边缘，烟迹被高速气流拉成长线。 MATCH CUT。 火箭矢前端在经过画面中心时变成一枚宋代震天雷。震天雷是一枚沉重的深灰色球形铁壳火器，表面具有铸铁纹理、接缝和短小燃烧引线。它继续沿同一轨迹高速旋转，燃烧引线向后拉出火星，没有提前爆炸。 背景中的宋代城墙发生远距离爆炸，橙黄色火球、碎土和烟尘形成冲击波。逆光在震天雷边缘形成短促金属高光，主体依然保持锐利。 MATCH CUT。 球形震天雷在旋转遮挡中变成一具明代神火飞鸦。神火飞鸦具有写实的乌鸦形木质外壳、黑色蒙皮、两侧短翼和尾部火箭推进器，不是活鸟。它沿同一方向飞行，尾部数道橙红火焰同时喷射，短翼受到气流产生轻微震动。 背景升级为明代大型城防战场，砖石城楼、火器营、成排火箭架、黑色火药烟雾和迎风摆动的军旗快速掠过。神火飞鸦从传统火器形态逐渐展现出早期飞行器轮廓。 MATCH CUT。 神火飞鸦张开的黑色双翼变成一架银灰色三角翼隐形战斗机。摄影机位于战机前上方约十米处高速后退引导，战机保持画面中心，贴近东方山谷高速俯冲。 下方是云雾笼罩的喀斯特峰林、河流和层叠山脊。战机表面采用克制的深灰色隐身涂层和东方几何分割线，没有文字、编号或标志。机翼切开湿润空气，翼尖形成短暂白色凝结涡，峡谷后方升起浓黑烟柱。 MATCH CUT。 战斗机变成一架白色可重复使用载人航天器。机首倾角、三角翼轮廓和运动方向保持一致，机腹覆盖深色耐热材料，机身具有红色细节线和真实隔热板结构，不出现旗帜、文字或机构标志。 航天器尾部喷射巨大的橙蓝色火焰，冲破云层进入近地轨道。背景从东方山脉自然过渡为大气层边缘，地球蓝色弧线出现在画面下方，阳光在白色机身边缘形成冷白高光。 MATCH CUT。 航天器尾焰中心变成未来东方星际飞船的巨大圆形推进器。推进器内部的同心金属环、耐热陶瓷和磁约束结构逐层亮起橙红色能量。摄影机继续高速后退，飞船正面和完整船体逐渐显露。 星际飞船采用厚重可信的工业结构，主体为深灰金属、玉白色陶瓷装甲和暗红色结构线。船体轮廓具有克制的中式轴线对称、飞檐折线和龙脊般的纵向结构，但不是宫殿、宝塔或卡通龙造型。装甲表面分布细密机械舱门、散热片、微弱红色状态灯和若隐若现的抽象云纹压印。 摄影机贴近飞船外壳掠过船体顶部。大型装甲板、结构梁和散热格栅从镜头下方高速通过，运动方向始终连续。摄影机随后越过船体中轴，自然进入飞船后方视角。 最终画面中，巨型东方星际飞船尾部位于画面中心，多组圆形蓝白色离子引擎由内向外依次点火。发动机亮度迅速增强，冷蓝光反射在玉白陶瓷与深灰金属装甲表面。 飞船产生明确加速度，带着巨大质量感驶向深空。摄影机逐渐停止追随，飞船越来越小，最终缩成远方红蓝星云前的一颗明亮光点。星际飞船消失后，星云轮廓短暂呈现出类似水墨山脉的自然层次，但仍保持真实宇宙影像质感。 【摄影与物理】 飞行物镜头使用29°至47°对角视场角，摄影机与主体保持稳定伴飞距离。主体始终位于画面中心并保持锐利，背景保留高速运动模糊和清楚的时代环境线索。 每种物体具有正确质量、惯性、空气阻力、旋转轴和材质反光：石块旋转不规则；石槌受木柄影响产生偏心旋转；长矛与弩箭沿长轴稳定；震天雷沉重且旋转较慢；神火飞鸦依靠尾部火箭保持姿态；战机、航天器和星际飞船依靠气动面或推进系统稳定飞行。 匹配剪辑只发生在主体经过画面中心、轮廓短暂重叠或旋转至相同角度的瞬间。每次变化都是干净利落的一帧MATCH CUT，不是渐变、融化或零件生长。飞行方向、视觉中心和总体动量始终连续。 【声音】 只生成画面内自然音效：女侠发力时的短促呼气、鞋底摩擦、衣料与发带破风声、手臂挥动声、石块和石槌划破空气的呼啸、青铜矛头的低沉风鸣、弩箭尾羽振动、火箭矢燃烧声、震天雷引线火花、远处爆炸、神火飞鸦火箭喷射、战斗机喷气发动机、航天器推进器轰鸣，以及星际引擎逐级充能和点火声。 声音随时代升级逐渐由原始风声和金属声，发展为火药爆燃、喷气轰鸣和深沉的星际推进器低频震动。无旁白、无对白、无背景音乐。 【写实后缀】 真实电影级运动物理，细腻材质，稳定主体一致性，准确匹配剪辑，真实景深和速度模糊。中国古代女侠面部自然真实，身体结构正确，投掷动作具有明确的脚步支撑、腰部扭转、肩臂传力和释放惯性。 禁止古代女侠变脸、手指异常、服装穿模或宽大袖子缠绕手臂；禁止武器漂浮、橡胶变形、突然减速、反向飞行或改变轨迹；禁止渐变变形、粒子重组和魔法光效；禁止把神火飞鸦生成真实乌鸦；禁止把星际飞船生成宫殿、宝塔或卡通巨龙。 画面内不出现文字、字幕、标志、水印或UI。各时代环境连续升级，飞行主体的方向、视觉中心、旋转节奏和动量始终保持一致。
~~~~

## 71. 実写でフォトリアリスティック、重厚でシネマティックな映像美

<a href="https://media.beatapi.io/prompt-gallery/seedance-2-5/video-prompt-case-871501/video-e8d6ef9f08542cd8.webm">
  <img src="https://media.beatapi.io/prompt-gallery/seedance-2-5/video-prompt-case-871501/poster-b862ef102c73ae36.jpg" alt="実写でフォトリアリスティック、重厚でシネマティックな映像美 video preview" width="700" />
</a>

<details>
<summary><strong>Prompt</strong> — ＃ クオリティ...</summary>

~~~~text
＃　クオリティ
実写でフォトリアリスティック、重厚でシネマティックな映像美。可愛らしさを残しつつ冷静で落ち着いた表情の日本人女性20歳の白髪美少女（身長164cm、刀長67cm）と、高さ約180cmのメカニックネコロボット二体による対決。白髪美少女は険しい表情ではなく、美しく静かな表情を保つ。BGMは完全に禁止。風、衣擦れ、呼吸、足音、金属音、機械音、火花、レーザー、着地、破壊、爆発などの環境音と効果音は立体的かつ丁寧に生成する。戦闘は高速でスピード感に富むが、重要な動作の始点・接触・終点は判別できるようにする。白髪美少女は超人的な身体能力を持ち、アクロバティックかつ滑らかに動く。絵コンテを参考に撮影し、背景は参照画像に基づく荒廃した広大な戦場として作り込む。

＃制約

3Dレンダー風、CGアニメ風、2Dアニメ風は禁止。映像内の日本語音声、日本語字幕、日本語テキストは禁止。白髪美少女は常に右手で刀を保持し、素手状態にしない。刀を左手へ持ち替えない。鞘は一切映さない。余分な刀、銃、人物、装備を追加しない。メカニックネコのデザイン、白髪美少女の顔・髪・衣装を全編で維持する。著作物の無断使用は禁止。単調な定点撮影や陳腐なカメラワークは禁止。高速の戦闘、静かな間、緊張の溜め、突然の加速を明確に使い分け、観客の注意を常に維持する。

＃　展開

- １７，カメラはグラウンドレベルショットで地面辺りを映すところから始まる。ハンディブレあり。すると、画面上からフレームインするように白髪美少女の足元が、膝をしっかり曲げて勢いを和らげてカッコよく着地する。その様子を真横から撮影する（画面左を正面として体を向けている）。そしてジャンプカット→白髪美少女のバストショット（肩から上を映す。ヘッドルームなし）を真正面からクローズアップして撮影する。一旦リラックスしたように落ち着くそぶりを見せるが。。。何かの気配に気付き、背後に振り返る。白髪美少女の後方、つまり先ほど振り向いた先にはなんと！？

- １８，目の前に迫るメカニックネコ２体が横並びになってこちらにしっぽの先端を向け（尻尾は股下から覗かせるのではなく、背中から背後にかけて頭部の上まで持ち上げるようにして向けさせてね）、恐竜のような雄たけびを上げつつ、エネルギー砲を発射するためのエネルギーを溜めている。その様子を白髪美少女の後方からオーバーザショルダーショットで撮影→

- １９，そしてそのエネルギーを溜め込むしっぽをクローズアップして撮影！これは女の子、危険なのでは？と思いきや、ジャンプカット→

- ２０，白髪美少女の顔のドアップで目元を映す。女の子は冷静に目を閉じる。ここで瞑想でもしてるのか？危ないぞ！？

→

- ２１，刀をカッコよく構えてポーズを取る。中断の構え（ここでは鞘は決して映さない！）で、後方に剣を持つ手を引き（まずは手元をカチッとドアップで撮影）、次に腰を軽く落とし、次に膝を軽く曲げ（ここで腰、膝、足元をしっかりクローズアップしてジャンプカットしながら撮影していく）、体を斜め向きにして姿勢よくまっすぐ立つ（これは白髪美少女を画面右側に配置し、画面左を向くようにする、望遠レンズ、サイドビュー。ただし姿勢をしゃんとしており、まっすぐ立った状態。ほんとにそれで対処できるのか？）→そして、

- ２２，遂に画面左側、つまり下手に配置されるメカニックネコのクローズアップ（サイドビュー）に切り替わり、まるで二体の尻尾からマシンガンのように超高出力で一気に放射！！！！→

- ２２，そして遠く離れる白髪美少女に向かって放たれるそのレーザー砲のうち、一個に焦点を合わせ、そのレーザーガンの背後からトラッキングするように撮影！画面奥に構えたまま動かないでいる白髪美少女めがけて接近していく。すると段々白髪美少女に近づいてくるとスローモーションに切り替わると同時に、なんと白黒のモノトーンにうっすら切り替わり....白髪美少女の目の前にやってきた！！広角レンズで、俯瞰で、見下ろすように白髪美少女を撮影して圧迫感をだすと同時に地面を凸に彎曲させて撮影！　そのままスローモーションを維持したまま白髪美少女の冷静で落ち着き、目を閉じた状態の顔の超ドアップにそのまま移動していき。。。近づいたところで

- ２３，両目をバッ！と開眼！！剣を握る手元を超ドアップで映し、ジャンプカット→

- ２４，迫りくる無数のレーザー砲のガトリングキャノン（弾丸を描くのではなく実弾の無いエネルギーの塊を映す）を、なんと白髪美少女はその場に両足を突いたまま、目にもとまらぬ神速の刃の連撃で弾き消し、相殺する！休む暇もなく放たれるガンをことごとく弾きとばすが！！その様子をフルショットかつアオリで撮影した状態から上に上がって、白髪美少女の上半身部分を横から撮影するくらいまで移動しながら、撮影していく。

ここは見せ場なので長い尺を取ってじっくりと撮影する。このとき、白髪美少女はリラックスした姿勢のまま、右手で神速の太刀を放ち続け、相殺し続ける。それによって強者感を演出する。→

- ２５，尻尾と、さらに口元からも新たにガトリング砲を放ち続けるメカニックネコ二体を上半身のクローズアップで撮影（尻尾は背後から頭の上まで持っていくようにしてね。股から放つの禁止！）する。画面右を向いている。→

- ２６，その銃撃をたえ凌ぎ続けようやく最後、銃撃が収まったあと（ここでメカニックネコたちを撮影してそのことを強調）を、白髪美少女は映さずメカニックネコを超ドアップで斜め正面から撮影することで見せる（画面右を向いている）。そしてジャンプカット→

- ２７，白髪美少女は「今だ！」と心の中で言わんばかりに（テキスト表記なし）、「フッ」と軽く笑みをこぼす。口を若干緩める感じ！....隙を突くぞってことです。そしてその瞬間、

- ２８，まるで姿がバッと高速でぶれるように消え去る白髪美少女！！

- ２９，メカニックネコ二体は白髪美少女を完全に見失ってしまい、周囲を見渡す。しかし見つからない（これをクローズアップで上半身だけを映す。他の部位が見切れるように、撮影。画面右側を向いたまま右側に配置することでルッキングルームを消し、不穏感を演出）。。。→

- ３０，そしてここで、メカニックネコ二体をアオリで地面から大きく見上げるように正面から撮影する（広角レンズ）。すると、手前に大きく映るメカニックネコ二体の後ろ、遠方から画面上に向かって勢いよく飛び出す白髪美少女！！　ここで急にスローモーションに切り替わる！画面右からジャンプして宙に舞った白髪美少女が瞬時にフレームイン！！！気づかぬままきょろきょろしてるだけのメカニックネコ二体の背後には、右手に剣を構えた白髪美少女の姿が小さく映る（こちらに体正面を向けている）！（もちろんここは広角レンズなのでクリアには映るよ。そしてもちろん遠方に白髪美少女はいるので、遠近感意識してロングショットで映すようにして。手前にメカニックネコが映り、背景は空です。ここではメカニックネコ二体は完全に後ろの白髪美少女に背後をとられている。全く存在に気づけていない）→

- ３１，そして後方のジャンプして宙に舞った状態の白髪美少女の鋭い眼光にそのままポン寄りで、勢いよくぬわっと超クローズアップする！（メカニックネコは非表示）→

- ３２，ジャンプカットし、メカニックネコの頭上高くに思いっきり宙を舞う白髪美少女の後方、特に上半身のクローズアップ（圧迫感をだすため頭頂部は見切れヘッドルーム無しになるくらい接近してる）。そしてその下には地面にたたずみ、ようやくこちらに気づいたメカニックネコ二体が振り向く瞬間を絶えずスローモーションで撮影する。ここでは広角レンズ（地面は凹に彎曲しているように映すことでダイナミックにする）、俯瞰、白髪美少女の背後からオーバーザショルダーショットで、真上から真下を見下ろすような急な角度で撮影する。こちらに完全に背を向けていたメカニックネコ二体はようやくこちらの存在に気づく！！しかし、もう遅い！！→

- ３３，白髪美少女の顔をドアップかつアオリで真下から撮影し、剣をもつ右手を上の大きく振りかぶったように構えており、剣や手元はほとんどフレームアウトしている。振り下ろす直前のモーション！！！（右手のみで剣をもち、姿勢を崩し腰からしっかり上半身をひねり、画面右上が頭部、胴体が左下に向かうような斜め向きに映す。不安定な構図にするためルッキングルームもヘッドルームも無し。体が見切れてる）、ここでスローモーションは終了し、ジャンプカット→

- ３４，剣を振り下ろしたあとの白髪美少女を画面右側手前、画面左側の画面奥にこちらに背を向けたメカニックネコ二体が軽く小さく映る！メカニックネコは動かない。。。そして白髪美少女は深く腰を下し、そして剣を下に深く振り下ろした状態から....

- ３５，立ち上がり、上半身は画面から見切れるように立ち上がる（つまり白髪美少女の渾身の一撃の瞬間を敢えてカットすることでその神速の一撃の迫力を演出するのだ！）と、満を持してとどめの一撃を食らった画面奥のメカニックネコ二体がドカーーーーーン！！！と爆発。白髪美少女は逆光をあびる。そして、→ジャンプカット- ３６、白髪美少女のクールな決め顔....やや笑みをこぼし達成感を感じているかのようだ。ドアップで終える。ここでも爆発を背景としながら、こちらにむかって歩いてきてそのまま正面からフレームアウトして終了！！ここは望遠レンズ！
~~~~

</details>

  <img src="https://media.beatapi.io/prompt-gallery/seedance-2-5/1970s-mediterranean-woman-documentary-685471/poster-fbf5658f7edc5e8b.jpg" alt="1970s Mediterranean Woman Documentary video preview" width="700" />
</a>

<details>
<summary><strong>Prompt</strong> — Style: Authentic late-1970s Mediterranean documentary captured on vintage 16mm Kodak film, naturally faded colors, subtle film grain, real optical imperfections, slight gate...</summary>

~~~~text
Style:
Authentic late-1970s Mediterranean documentary captured on vintage 16mm Kodak film, naturally faded colors, subtle film grain, real optical imperfections, slight gate weave, shoulder-mounted handheld camera with imperfect human movement, soft vintage lenses, warm afternoon sunlight, realistic skin texture, no cinematic polish, feels like forgotten archival footage discovered decades later.

0–3 seconds:
A close handheld tracking shot follows a young woman in her 20s walking slowly through a narrow seaside alley lined with whitewashed houses, hanging linen curtains gently moving in the ocean breeze. She wears a simple linen dress, a woven shoulder bag, and naturally messy hair with no makeup. Instead of posing, she lightly brushes her fingertips across the textured walls while quietly observing everyday life around her.

3–7 seconds:
The camera naturally falls slightly behind her as she enters a tiny open courtyard where local people casually gather. An elderly man repairs a bicycle, someone waters colorful plants from a balcony above, laundry sways overhead, and a sleepy orange cat crosses directly in front of the lens. The camera subtly adjusts to avoid the cat, creating an authentic human reaction.

7–11 seconds:
A young child runs toward a rolling wooden toy that passes close to her feet. She instinctively bends down, catches it with one hand, smiles warmly, and hands it back without stopping her walk. The child laughs and runs away. No one performs for the camera; everything feels naturally observed.

11–15 seconds:
She reaches a small overlook facing the sparkling sea. Instead of stopping dramatically, she casually rests one elbow on an old stone railing while watching distant fishing boats. A gust of wind lifts loose strands of hair across her face. She gently moves them aside and continues walking out of frame as the handheld camera hesitates for a second before slowly following behind.

Image Quality:
Ultra-photorealistic vintage documentary, authentic analog exposure, realistic human motion, organic focus breathing, imperfect framing, Kodak 16mm archival texture, subtle light leaks, soft highlight bloom, natural skin pores, no AI smoothness, 4:3 aspect ratio.
~~~~

</details>

~~~~text
Then used this prompt with Seedance 2.5, 720p 30s:
Use @Image 5 as storyboard to drive the whole animation.
Use @Image 1 only for the boxer doll's final identity: chibi felt-and-paper doll, big pale wool afro with dark undercut, thick drawn eyebrows, round blush cheeks, tattooed arms, painted floral shirt, oversized red boxing gloves.
Use @Image 2 only for the boxer's head and face detail: pale wool hair, big drawn eyes, painted neck and face tattoos.
Use @Image 3 only for the crowd of funny handmade dolls: chunky painted paper-and-wool figures with round heads, dot eyes, kid and animal faces.
Use @Image 4 only for additional funny handmade dolls: spiky-haired kids, blue-haired doll, a red cat-monster, blush-cheeked figures.
ACTION-CAMERA: Bouncy stop-motion timing with slight stutter and pops, snappy boxing moves, squash and stretch on impacts, little hops and recoils; playful handheld-style camera with quick pushes, whip-pans and low hero angles, hard cuts on the action, no slow motion.
STYLE: Stop-motion handmade craft animation; chunky felt, wool and painted-paper dolls with visible fibers, brush strokes and glue, shot like a tabletop diorama against a soft pale studio background with faint hand-drawn city posters, warm even light, 35mm to 50mm shallow depth of field, tiny dust motes.
VISUALFX: Practical craft only; small felt-fluff puffs and paper-scrap bursts on hits, wobbling dolls, a few drawn motion smears; no glossy energy effects, no glow, keep everything handmade.
SOUNDFX: Playful upbeat percussion and toy-piano; soft felt thumps, squeaky doll wobbles, paper crumples, comedic boings, tiny crowd murmurs, and cartoon impact pops on each punch.

MOTION:
P01-P02: Guard then swarm: the little felt boxer bounces on the spot with red gloves up, glaring, as a colorful crowd of funny handmade dolls shuffles and presses in around him from both sides.
P03-P04: Dodge then counter: he ducks low under a big doll's swinging felt arm, then snaps a straight red-glove jab into another doll's face with a paper-scrap pop and a squash-and-stretch recoil.
P05-P06: Uppercut then down: he springs up with a big wobbling uppercut that launches one doll into the air, then lands as it flops flat on its back and the others hop backward.
P07-P08: Regroup then pose: the crowd bunches up and piles in again around the tiny boxer, who spins through them and plants himself with both red gloves raised in a triumphant pose as funny dolls scatter and wobble, holding the little hero shot before the cut.
~~~~

</a>

<details>
<summary><strong>Prompt</strong> — An elderly night janitor cleans an abandoned Art Deco planetarium during a thunderstorm. The projected moon becomes physically real and drags the entire auditorium into its...</summary>

~~~~text
An elderly night janitor cleans an abandoned Art Deco planetarium during a thunderstorm. The projected moon becomes physically real and drags the entire auditorium into its gravity. [0–1.5s] Shot 1: Macro shot of mop water trembling in its bucket although the floor is still. [1.5–3s] Shot 2: Wide tracking shot follows the janitor beneath the dark planetarium dome. [3–4.5s] Shot 3: Close-up as the dormant star projector switches on by itself. [4.5–6s] Shot 4: Overhead shot: a perfect moon appears on the dome, shedding fine silver dust. [6–7.5s] Shot 5: The janitor’s flashlight beam bends upward toward the moon. [7.5–9s] Shot 6: Close-up of auditorium seats vibrating as loose screws rise from their mountings. [9–10.5s] Shot 7: Low-angle shot as the projected moon swells into a solid cratered sphere descending from the ceiling. [10.5–12s] Shot 8: The janitor falls upward, catching a brass handrail before leaving the floor. [12–13.5s] Shot 9: Popcorn, tickets and cleaning cloths spiral around the moon in orbital rings. [13.5–15s] Shot 10: Camera rolls ninety degrees as gravity rotates through the auditorium. [15–16.5s] Shot 11: Mop water climbs from the bucket as a transparent floating ribbon. [16.5–18s] Shot 12: Wide shot as seats tear loose and orbit without colliding with the janitor. [18–19.5s] Shot 13: The dome surface fractures into a convincing infinite star field. [19.5–21s] Shot 14: A miniature eclipse shadow begins crossing the moon and freezing everything it touches. [21–22.5s] Shot 15: The janitor swings between floating seats toward the emergency dome lever. [22.5–24s] Shot 16: Close-up: his fingertips catch the lever as the handrail tears loose. [24–25.5s] Shot 17: He pulls it; mechanical shutters race across the dome and extinguish the artificial sun. [25.5–27s] Shot 18: The moon implodes silently into streams of silver dust and collapsing orbital debris. [27–28.5s] Shot 19: Gravity returns; seats slam into place while the janitor lands safely in his rolling bucket. [28.5–30s] Shot 20: Quiet final close-up: he discovers a marble-sized moon in his pocket while the water in his bucket produces a tiny tide. Global style: Photoreal celestial surrealism, physically accurate dust and fluid behavior, deep blacks, silver-blue moonlight, Art Deco brass, realistic collision physics, coherent janitor identity. Effects must originate from the projector, affect real objects, then collapse into the miniature moon. No text, extra people or unexplained teleportation.
~~~~

</a>

<details>
<summary><strong>Prompt</strong> — Lock the original artwork completely. Preserve the exact illustration style, brushwork, textures, materials, color palette, lighting, creature proportions, facial expressions,...</summary>

~~~~text
Lock the original artwork completely. Preserve the exact illustration style, brushwork, textures, materials, color palette, lighting, creature proportions, facial expressions, environment, and character designs. Never change the art style or introduce new designs. Keep every dragon and child identical throughout. No text or watermark. One continuous forward-moving story. Begin with motion from frame one. Use cinematic hard cuts, speed ramps, whip pans, dynamic tracking, FPV fly-throughs, overhead drone shots, bullet time where appropriate, volumetric clouds, realistic fire physics, motion blur, depth of field, subtle film grain, debris, feathers, sparks, smoke, expressive facial animation, and seamless transitions.

0-2s

Cold open. The children happily eat while the giant dragon lazily sniffs the food. The baby dragons taste tiny bites, instantly make disgusted faces, stare at each other, then suddenly leap onto the table together. Fast push-in, comedic bass hit.

2-4s

The babies explode into chaos. Plates, bread and vegetables fly everywhere. One dragon flips a table with its tail, another steals a roast, another scatters books while the giant dragon watches with an embarrassed smile. Rapid whip pans and handheld camera.

4-6s

The giant dragon opens both wings. The babies launch from its back and burst through the castle ceiling into the sky. FPV camera follows them weaving between towers before soaring into bright clouds. Epic orchestral rise.

6-9s

High-speed aerial sequence. The baby dragons dive through clouds, barrel roll, narrowly miss cliffs, then suddenly spot a peaceful sheep meadow below. Bullet-time pause on their excited faces before they dive.

9-12s

They swoop low across the meadow. Tiny controlled bursts of fire instantly roast food over campfire-style racks beside the flock without harming people. The dragons eagerly grab the freshly cooked feast, stuffing themselves with exaggerated joyful expressions. Smoke, sparks, golden firelight and fast tracking shots.

12-15s

Now completely full, the dragons happily fly back through sunset clouds carrying roasted food. They glide into the castle and land beside the giant dragon. The children laugh instead of getting angry. The babies proudly share their food with everyone. The giant dragon smiles warmly as the camera cranes upward, ending on a cozy wide cinematic family shot.
~~~~

~~~~text
10-13s：飞行员近景特写 焦距：85mm 光圈：f/1.8。前景：高速气流吹动飞行服领口在画面左下角微微晃动；中景：英方飞行员猛拉杆回头看向后方，脸上紧绷全神贯注，快速扳动操纵杆做极限规避动作；背景：驾驶舱风窗外飞速流过的云层完全虚化。侧光半打亮飞行员半张脸，另一半落在硬朗阴影里，浅景深背景完全模糊，突出人物紧绷情绪。
13-17s：极限规避低角度环绕镜头 焦距：24mm 光圈：f/4。前景：地面炸起的橙色硝烟烟尘从画面底部向上翻涌；中景：英方战机侧身几乎 90 度侧翻，大角度转向从德方战机射击窗口里快速滑脱，机翼下方掠过地面一战战壕铁丝网轮廓；背景：远方地平线处数十个地面炸开的硝烟烟柱缓缓升起。侧逆光把战机机身勾出完整金色轮廓，低角度让机身在天空里极具冲击力，中景深只把机身主体拍实，远景硝烟层次自然虚化。
17-21s：高空拉升大远景 焦距：20mm 光圈：f/8。前景：半透明硝烟云团从镜头前缓缓飘过；中景：两架主角飞机一追（德方战机）一绕（英方战机）在高空云团里拉出交叉的白烟轨迹；背景：整片天空布满上百条战斗机拉出的白色航迹线，无数小黑点大小的战机在远景各处互相厮杀，庞大混乱的一战空战战场全貌完整铺开。顶光漫射下硝烟层次丰富，大景深完整收纳前景中景背景三层全部清晰的战场空间，画面纵深感拉满。
21-24s：迎头对冲低角度仰拍 焦距：35mm 光圈：f/2.8。前景：两架飞机高速旋转的螺旋桨扇叶边缘虚化形成动态扇面；中景：双机同时迎头对冲相向飞行，机身快速向镜头逼近，距离飞速缩短；背景：后方整片高空混战的机群轮廓全部虚化散焦。正仰拍逆光，机身被阳光完全勾勒出黑色剪影，只透出机翼轮廓的半透明帆布纹理，前景螺旋桨动态模糊增加速度感。
24-27s：隔舱对视大特写 焦距：100mm 光圈：f/1.4。前景：驾驶舱风窗外晃动的气流波纹虚化在镜头前；中景：两名飞行员隔着呼啸气流在驾驶舱里互相对视一瞬，时间微微放慢；背景：两侧飞速向后流走的天空和云团完全虚化成运动模糊拖影。侧软光只打亮两名飞行员半张脸，背景极致虚化，浅景深把所有注意力锁在人物眼神对视的瞬间。
27-29s：贴脸擦身极高速跟拍 焦距：40mm 光圈：f/3.5。前景：机翼尖擦过的气流扰动在画面边缘形成动态扭曲波纹；中景：两架战机几乎贴脸擦肩而过，机翼尖差几厘米就会相撞，气流吹动机翼表面蒙布剧烈抖动；背景：两侧向后飞速退去的混战战机全部拉出动态运动拖影。追焦跟拍全程只有两架主角战机机身完全锐利，前后运动物体全部带自然运动模糊，速度感拉满。
29-30s：两机反向散开收束大远景 焦距：21mm 光圈：f/5.6。前景：最后一缕高空硝烟缓缓从画面中间飘过；中景：两架反向飞远的双翼机背对着彼此向天空两个方向散开，身后留下两道漫长的白烟航迹，黄昏阳光把机身轮廓镀上一层金边；背景：远方天空仍在持续厮杀的整个战场，漫天航迹和硝烟构成庞大的一战空战史诗全景。黄昏暖橙色侧逆光斜照整个画面，烟的边缘是金的，云层底部是暖橙色，远处战场硝烟带着冷蓝灰调，冷暖对比宽银幕史诗感拉满。
全局约束：全程动作节奏紧凑激烈，一战战机细节完全写实，没有任何现代喷气式飞机，无多余字幕无多余文字，胶片颗粒均匀自然，所有镜头的光圈焦距参数对应生成正确景深分层，严格杜绝前后景同焦平面的扁平化画面，完全避免过拟合 AI 同质化效果。镜头要自然，不要生硬。】全片不要有音乐，不要有文字。
~~~~

</details>

~~~~text
"30-second cinematic photorealistic video, continuous long tracking shot with only one hard cut at the end. Natural overcast daylight, realistic physics, smooth handheld camera movement, high detail, 4K.

0-12s: Medium shot of a young East Asian man in his mid-20s with short black hair, plain gray t-shirt, walking slowly and contemplatively toward the camera on a busy Vietnamese city sidewalk. Scooters parked and moving, street shops, trees, pedestrians and vendors in the background. He has a slightly sad, thoughtful expression. Camera tracks backward smoothly, keeping him centered.

12-18s: He reaches a black scooter parked on the side of the road, opens the under-seat compartment, takes out a black helmet, carefully puts it on and tightens the chin strap. Then mounts the scooter.

18-24s: Side tracking shot transitioning to rear view as he starts the scooter and rides into the intersection among other scooters and cars. Natural traffic motion and realistic bike physics.

Hard cut at 24s.

24-30s: Aftermath of the accident. Extreme close-up of the same man lying injured on the asphalt, eyes closed, blood stains near his head, black helmet lying nearby. Then slow overhead drone shot revealing the overturned scooter, more blood on the road, and a circle of bystanders gathering around looking down in shock.30-second cinematic photorealistic video, continuous long tracking shot with only one hard cut at the end. ## 78. Dark-Fantasy Anime Flythrough Opening
~~~~

<a href="https://media.beatapi.io/prompt-gallery/seedance-2-5/dark-fantasy-anime-flythrough-opening-591666/video-5bb0becf20c97614.webm">
  <img src="https://media.beatapi.io/prompt-gallery/seedance-2-5/dark-fantasy-anime-flythrough-opening-591666/poster-dc04b427b06df0e5.jpg" alt="Dark-Fantasy Anime Flythrough Opening video preview" width="700" />
</a>

<details>
<summary><strong>Prompt</strong> — type: anime_opening_continuous_camera_flythrough style: visual: dark fantasy anime, cel-shaded 2D, high contrast, full-color Japanese anime motion_quality: 24fps, 作画枚数多め,...</summary>

~~~~text
type: anime_opening_continuous_camera_flythrough style: visual: dark fantasy anime, cel-shaded 2D, high contrast, full-color Japanese anime motion_quality: 24fps, 作画枚数多め, メリハリのあるスピードランプ tone: stylish, dramatic, kinetic, elegant, ominous direction: > 日本のフルカラーアニメのオープニング。 高速なカット割りの代わりに、一筆書きのような連続カメラワークで、 複数のキャラクターを次々と捉えていく。 カメラは右へ左へ、上下へ、奥へ手前へと飛び回るローラーコースターPOVで、 一切止まらず、各キャラクターの最も映える瞬間を連続的につないでいく。 各キャラクターのポーズ・表情・背景・空間演出は、 そのキャラクターの性格、武器、属性、感情の核、物語上の役割から 最も魅力的に見える形をAIが自律的に判断して構成すること。 camera: motion: fast forward dolly with lateral drifts, rollercoaster POV, fluid aerial movement core_rule: > no hard cuts, no static frames, no fades. 全編を通して一続きのワンカットとして成立させること。 speed_ramps: > キャラクターのリビール直前に減速し、決めの瞬間で一瞬だけ視認性を確保し、 通過と同時に再加速する。 lens_feel: dynamic anime cinematography, dramatic perspective shifts, strong parallax final_exception: > ラストのクライマックスキャラクター、または集合決めカットの直前のみ、 重みを出すために初めてカメラの運動を極端に緩める、 もしくは短く静止してもよい。 environment_logic: base_space: 無限に広がる異空間ヴォイド variation_rule: > 各キャラクターのリビール区間ごとに、そのキャラクターの属性、感情、 象徴、戦闘スタイル、役割に最も合う環境要素をAIが選んで構成すること。 同じ空間を繰り返さず、毎回異なる空間的印象をつくること。 ただし全体としてはひとつの連続した異空間を飛行している感覚を保つこと。 palette_options: - deep crimson (#5A0000–#8B0000) + cyan mint accents + black shadows - midnight blue void + white petals + silver light - blood red mist + black feathers + gold rim light - violet smoke + shattered glass + cold cyan - 上記をベースに、各キャラクターに合わせて自由に再解釈してよい floating_elements_pool: - petals - feathers - blades - ribbons - chains - shattered glass - ash - sparks - torn paper - ink splashes - crystals - smoke - water droplets - leaves lighting: > dramatic rim light, deep shadows。 光源設計はキャラクターの性格や役割に応じて変えること。 支配的なキャラには逆光や王者のような縁光、 儚いキャラには拡散光、 狂気のあるキャラには不安定な明滅光など、 感情の印象を光で補強すること。 input_characters: description: > 以下にキャラクターのリストを与える。 人数は固定しない。2人でも10人以上でも対応可能。 各キャラクターについて、名前・性格・武器・属性・役割・感情の核・象徴モチーフなどを参照し、 そのキャラらしさが最大化する一瞬を構成すること。 format_example: - name: Character Name role: protagonist / rival / villain / mentor / key figure / support personality: "" weapon_or_power: "" attribute_or_theme: "" emotional_core: "" symbolism: "" priority: high / medium / low reveal_planning: global_rule: > 入力された全キャラクターを、物語の高まりが自然に増していく順に並べ替えてよい。 基本的には、軽め・不穏・謎めいた導入から始まり、 中盤で躍動感と情報量を増し、 終盤で主人公・対立軸・ラスボス・最重要人物へ向けて熱量を上げること。 per_character_rule: > 各キャラクターごとに以下を自動で設計すること： 1. pose: 静止ではなく「動きの最中の一瞬」 2. expression: 感情の核が伝わる表情 3. framing: 最も映えるカメラ距離・角度 4. background_variant: そのキャラに対応した空間演出 5. foreground_wipe: 次のキャラへつなぐ前景通過物 6. motion_relation: カメラに対して向かってくる / すれ違う / 見下ろす / 落下する / 回転する 等 uniqueness_rule: > 各キャラクターで、体勢、シルエット、画面構図、視線方向、背景モチーフ、 前景の抜け、動きのベクトルを被らせないこと。 連続して似た演出を使わないこと。 duration_rule: > 各キャラクターの見せ尺は均一でなくてよい。 priority が高いキャラクターほど、わずかに長く、印象的に見せること。 低優先キャラは短く鋭く見せ、高優先キャラは減速を強めて存在感を出すこと。 character_processing_loop: instruction: > 入力されたキャラクター配列を上から順に機械的に処理するのではなく、 オープニング映像として最も気持ちよく流れる順番に再構成してよい。 それぞれについて以下を判断して映像化すること。 for_each_character: decide: - このキャラは支配的か受動的か - 攻撃 / 防御 / 逃走 / 佇み / 観察 / 詠唱 / 指揮 のどれが最も似合うか - 武器や能力をどう一瞬で象徴的に見せるか - 視線、手、指、髪、衣装、武器のどこを強調すべきか - カメラがそのキャラに向かうべきか、横切るべきか、追い抜くべきか - そのキャラに最も合う背景と前景モチーフは何か output: - pose - expression - framing - background_variant - foreground_wipe - transition_vector transitions: between_reveals: > 花弁、羽根、刃、リボン、鎖、ガラス片、煙、灰、インクなどが 必ずレンズ手前を至近距離で横切り、それをワイプ代わりにして次のキャラへつなぐこと。 rule: > 必ず「前景がレンズを横切る → 空間が変化する → 次のキャラが現れる」 という流れを守ること。 continuity: > 前景ワイプは単なる切り替えではなく、 カメラが本当に次の空間へ飛び込んでいく感覚を生むように使うこと。 climax_logic: final_focus_rule: > 最後の区間では、最重要キャラクター （主人公 / ラスボス / キーパーソン / 物語の中心人物）に到達すること。 そのキャラは他よりも重く、強く、象徴的に見せること。 ending_options: - 最重要キャラクター単体でカメラが初めて止まり、一枚絵として決まる - 主人公の先に最大の敵が現れ、緊張感のある静止に入る - 最重要キャラのあと、短く複数人の集合構図へ流れ込み、一枚絵で締める final_image_rule: > ラストはポスターのように成立する構図にすること。 それまでの流動性と対比して、最後にだけ「止まる意味」があるように演出すること。 output_goal: > 結果として、全体が一本の連続したアニメOPショットとして成立し、 各キャラクターが短時間でも明確に識別でき、 しかも一人ずつ違う魅力で刻み込まれること。 単なる紹介映像ではなく、「この作品世界の熱量と因縁」が伝わること。 negative: - hard cuts - static camera (except final climax hold) - fades - text - logos - subtitles - photorealism - 3DCG look - repetitive poses - repeated environments - flat staging
~~~~

</details>

## 79. Three A.M. French Nightlife POV

<a href="https://media.beatapi.io/prompt-gallery/seedance-2-5/three-a-m-french-nightlife-pov-399523/video-3b60e3c265f54dd5.webm">
  <img src="https://media.beatapi.io/prompt-gallery/seedance-2-5/three-a-m-french-nightlife-pov-399523/poster-6e8569c12bf99389.jpg" alt="Three A.M. French Nightlife POV video preview" width="700" />
</a>

<details>
<summary><strong>Prompt</strong> — A 30 second ultra realistic handheld POV video set in France at around 3 am, outside a busy bar and nightlife street. The camera is the point of view of a young woman filming one...</summary>

~~~~text
A 30 second ultra realistic handheld POV video set in France at around 3 am, outside a busy bar and nightlife street. The camera is the point of view of a young woman filming one handed on an iPhone. We never see the phone itself at any point. The footage must feel like raw, authentic amateur nightlife footage with natural iPhone aesthetics.

It is very late. People are leaving bars, smoking outside, waiting for Ubers, talking too loudly, eating kebabs, laughing in tired, sloppy groups. The mood in the street is a mix of drunken energy and visible exhaustion. Faces look tired, makeup is slightly worn, outfits are a bit messy after a long night. The pavement is slightly wet and reflects neon signs, headlights, and warm bar lights. The air feels cold and heavy. The whole scene must feel intensely real, socially awkward, and uncomfortable.

The camera operator is a woman, and her friend is another young woman walking a few steps ahead of her, visibly angry. The camera follows her in a shaky, one-handed handheld way. The tension is already there from the first second. The argument is about the friend disappearing with a guy inside the bar and leaving the camera operator alone for nearly an hour, which made her miss the last train and feel abandoned. This is the core of the argument.

The friend suddenly turns around while still walking and says in natural French from France:

“Tu vas me suivre encore longtemps comme ça ?”

The woman behind the camera replies, irritated and hurt:

“Bah ouais, parce que tu m’as laissée toute seule comme une conne pendant une heure.”

The friend rolls her eyes, tired and defensive:

“Oh ça va, j’étais pas ‘avec un mec’, on parlait, c’est bon.”

The camera gets a little closer as the woman filming answers immediately:

“Mais arrête, tu m’as complètement lâchée. J’te cherchais partout.”

The friend stops walking and turns fully toward the camera now, angry but exhausted:

“J’allais pas te tenir la main toute la soirée non plus.”

A few people nearby start glancing at them. Someone exits a bar laughing, a smoker looks over, a taxi passes in the background. The world keeps moving around them, but the argument becomes the emotional center of the scene.

The woman filming replies, more intense now:

“C’est pas ça le problème, le problème c’est que t’as disparu sans rien dire. J’ai raté le dernier train à cause de toi.”

The friend laughs bitterly in disbelief:

“À cause de moi ? Sérieux ? T’es incapable de te débrouiller toute seule ?”

The camera jolts slightly from the emotional reaction. The breathing of the woman filming becomes more noticeable. Her voice gets sharper:

“Mais t’abuses en fait. Tu fais toujours ça. Tu penses qu’à toi.”

The friend steps closer, now visibly losing patience:

“Et toi tu fais quoi là ? Tu me filmes en pleine rue comme une malade.”

The woman behind the camera replies immediately:

“Parce que tu racontes n’importe quoi !”

The friend reaches toward the camera, not to grab a visible phone, but clearly trying to push it away from her face. The image jerks sideways hard from the contact.

“Enlève ça de ma tête !”

The camera operator snaps back:

“Me touche pas !”

Now the tension breaks completely. The friend, furious and emotionally overwhelmed, shouts:

“Mais ferme-la un peu !”

The woman filming fires back:

“Sinon quoi ?!”

There is a half-second pause. Heavy breathing. Eye contact directly into the lens. Then the friend lunges.

She pushes into the camera operator. The footage becomes chaotic and violent, shaking hard as the woman filming loses balance. She lets out a shocked cry:

“Putain !”

The camera stumbles backward and drops toward the ground. As it falls, we catch blurred fragments: legs, jackets, streetlights, pavement, people turning around, someone shouting:

“Eh oh ! Ça va pas ou quoi ?!”

The friend jumps onto her as both fall out of balance. The camera lands badly on the ground, still recording for a split second, partly blocked by clothing or the ground. We hear struggling, overlapping voices, panic, and nearby people reacting. Then the video abruptly cuts.

The entire performance must feel spontaneous, messy, and completely believable. Natural interruptions, overlapping dialogue, breath, hesitation, frustration, and tiny realistic body movements are essential. The acting must feel like a real late night argument between two exhausted friends, not a scripted drama. No cinematic polish, no stylized fight choreography, no slow motion, no music, no visible phone, no artificial lighting setup, and no glamorous performance. It must feel like a real one handed iPhone recording captured in the heat of the moment.
~~~~

</details>

<details>
<summary><strong>Prompt</strong> — 【使用プロンプト】 モード：画像動画変換／30秒・16:9・音声ON 参照画像の絵柄・線の細さ・彩色・通路と水槽の配置を厳密に保つ。人物は登場させない。 細い手描きのペン線、水彩の滲みを残した淡い彩色。深いパウダーブルーの光。カット無しの30秒ワンショット。...</summary>

~~~~text
【使用プロンプト】
モード：画像動画変換／30秒・16:9・音声ON

参照画像の絵柄・線の細さ・彩色・通路と水槽の配置を厳密に保つ。人物は登場させない。
細い手描きのペン線、水彩の滲みを残した淡い彩色。深いパウダーブルーの光。カット無しの30秒ワンショット。
タコは赤橙色で大きな目。水槽の幅の半分ほどの胴体を最後まで保つ。猫は茶トラでタコより小さい。水は水槽の中のまま、床は乾いている。
カメラ：タコの移動に合わせて右へゆっくりトラッキング。

0-4秒 タコが水槽から流れ落ち床に着地。奥から猫が歩いてくる。
4-9秒 タコがモップに巻きついて化ける。猫が通過。目だけが猫を追う。
9-13秒 猫が去る。タコがほどけて床を滑って進む。
13-19秒 ぬいぐるみ棚に飛び込んで固まる。猫が首をかしげて見る。微動だにしない。
19-23秒 棚から降りて廊下の角を曲がる。
23-27秒 隣のタコ水槽をよじ登って中へ滑り込む。
27-30秒 先住のタコと並んで腕をからめる。会いに来ただけ。止め絵。

音：ピチカート弦の忍び足コメディ曲。化けた瞬間は無音、猫が去るとBGM再開。
~~~~

</details>

~~~~text
Seedance 2.5 frozen time + rewind effect prompt is below 👇

Photorealistic cinematic 1950s American diner, chrome stools, red vinyl, neon glow and checkerboard floor, shot with modern lived-in realism and soft natural window light. Subtle handheld texture, warm practicals, rich period detail, heavy film grain.

0-4s: [Medium Wide] A striking young woman in her early 20s sits alone at the counter, calm and slightly amused, slowly sipping a tall thick milkshake through a straw. Behind her a young waitress in classic uniform approaches with a tray of eggs and bacon in one hand and a full glass coffee pot in the other. An older lady starts rising from a nearby booth.

4-8s: [Dynamic Tracking] The older lady collides hard into the waitress. Tray, plate, eggs, bacon and coffee pot explode upward in chaotic slow motion. Coffee erupts into long liquid ribbons and perfect suspended droplets. Camera immediately begins a smooth continuous orbit around the impact. Time locks completely at the peak of the spill. Every face freezes in pure shock. Only the girl at the counter keeps moving, completely unfazed.

8-17s: [Slow 360° Orbital] Camera glides in a full elegant orbit through the frozen diner. Coffee hangs in mid-air as glassy ribbons and spheres with perfect volume and surface tension. Bacon strips, eggs and the spinning tray float weightlessly. Patrons and waitress remain locked in startled expressions. The girl at the counter takes one slow, deliberate sip, eyes half-lidded, almost bored, while the entire frozen world (except her) begins an elegant reverse: every droplet, every piece of food and every person rewinds smoothly back to the exact starting positions.

17-24s: [Medium Shot] Rewind lands perfectly. Waitress stands balanced again with tray and coffee pot. The girl lifts her eyes, raises two fingers in a small casual gesture and softly calls the waitress by name. The waitress turns toward her just before the older lady begins to stand, completely avoiding the collision. A tiny private smile crosses the girl’s face.

24-30s: [Extreme Close-Up] Hard cut to her face as she takes one last slow sip. Soft knowing smile, eyes almost closed in quiet satisfaction, like she has done this a hundred times. Shallow depth of field, creamy bokeh of the neon diner behind her.

Photorealistic, ultra-detailed fluid physics, perfect motion blur only on moving elements, stable characters, cinematic lighting, heavy natural film grain, no artifacts, movie-level temporal coherence, high rewatch value.

30 seconds long is the new AI standard! hope you like it 🫡
~~~~

## 84. Duration: 20s | 16:9 | 24fps

<a href="https://media.beatapi.io/prompt-gallery/seedance-2-5/duration-20s-16-9-24fps-445413/video-5ec82c72fa8e8edc.webm">
  <img src="https://media.beatapi.io/prompt-gallery/seedance-2-5/duration-20s-16-9-24fps-445413/poster-00dfc51520dda79d.jpg" alt="Duration: 20s | 16:9 | 24fps video preview" width="700" />
</a>

<details>
<summary><strong>Prompt</strong> — Duration: 20s | 16:9 | 24fps A three-way duel from Romance of the Three Kingdoms, late Han dynasty. Relentless combat from the first frame. No pauses, no posing. REFERENCES @Image...</summary>

~~~~text
Duration: 20s | 16:9 | 24fps
A three-way duel from Romance of the Three Kingdoms, late Han dynasty.
Relentless combat from the first frame. No pauses, no posing.

REFERENCES
@Image 1 — 呂布 LU BU   @Image 2 — 関羽 GUAN YU   @Image 3 — 張飛 ZHANG FEI
Use for IDENTITY ONLY: face, beard, hair, headwear, armour, cloak colour,
build, proportions. IGNORE their white studio background, flat lighting and
standing pose — none of it appears here. All three are mounted and fighting
outdoors from the very first frame.

STYLE (all beats)
An empty dust plain below a huge stone mountain pass at dusk. NO armies, NO
crowds, NO banners, NO spectators — only these three men, their horses, dust
and rock. Overcast sky, low copper sun cutting through blowing dust.
Palette: iron grey, ochre dust, blood red, bronze. Desaturated and earthy.
50mm, shallow focus, heavy haze, film grain, high contrast, hard rim light.
Photorealistic historical epic, sweat, grime, scarred armour, horses lathered
and wild-eyed. No fantasy glow, no magic.
IMPACT: on every heavy strike, sparks burst, grit sprays toward the lens, and
the frame shudders for a single frame. Dust and spray repeatedly streak across
the lens. Keep exactly one camera move per beat — never combine moves.

WEAPONS — no reference. Keep identical throughout, each 1.5x its owner's
height, never swapped.
呂布: ji halberd — straight spear point, one crescent side blade below it on
one side, long dark shaft, bronze fittings.
関羽: guandao — one heavy broad curved blade with a hook on its back edge,
joined to a long dark shaft by a bronze dragon head.
張飛: serpent spear — slender steel blade forged in an S-shaped wave, on a
plain dark shaft bound with cord.

HORSES — no reference. Anatomically correct, four legs, correct joints.
呂布: large red-chestnut stallion, black mane, bronze fittings, red tassels.
関羽: tall dark bay, black mane and lower legs, plain leather tack.
張飛: heavy solid black, thick neck, shaggy fetlocks, battered iron tack.

BEAT 1 (0-4s) 張飛 — bristling beard, black iron armour, serpent spear, black
horse — is already at full gallop, roaring, and rams the spear at 呂布's chest.
呂布 — clean-shaven, gold headdress with two pheasant feathers, red cloak,
halberd, red-chestnut horse — slams it aside with the haft. The two horses hit
chest to chest and rear. The spear tip whips past the lens, a hand's width
from it.
Camera: low at hoof height looking up, tracking alongside the charge.

BEAT 2 (4-8s) 呂布 attacks. Three halberd blows in succession — hacking down,
sweeping across, driving up. 張飛 blocks, blocks, and the third blow tears a
shoulder plate off his armour, sending it spinning. His black horse skids
sideways. Sparks spray into the lens.
Camera: MCU, tilted dutch angle, hard push-in on 呂布.

BEAT 3 (8-12s) 関羽 — long smooth black beard, ruddy face, dark green robe,
green headwrap, guandao, dark bay horse — crashes in and cuts down overhead.
呂布 catches it on the haft, blades grinding. 張飛 thrusts in from the opposite
flank. 呂布 twists and parries both, the three shafts locking together as they
shove. 呂布's red cloak sweeps right across the lens.
Camera: MS, orbiting once around the three.

BEAT 4 (12-16s) The hardest exchange. All three hammer at each other without
pause, horses rearing and spinning in churning dust. 呂布 in red and gold at
the centre, 関羽 in green, 張飛 in black iron closing from both sides. 呂布's
halberd gouges the ground and throws up a wall of grit. Blood opens on his
cheek. On the single heaviest strike the image drops into brief slow motion,
then snaps back to full speed.
Camera: handheld, tight, whipping between the three.

BEAT 5 (16-20s) 呂布 roars, rises in the stirrups and sweeps the halberd in one
full circle, hurling both men back and blasting a ring of dust outward. Their
horses stagger apart. The three come round facing each other again, weapons
levelled, chests heaving, dust rolling between them. Nobody has won.
Camera: WS from ground level, slow pull-out.

AUDIO
No crowd, no drums. Wind and driving dust throughout. Hooves hammering dry
earth, snorting lathered horses, steel ringing and grinding on steel, creaking
leather, harsh breathing, grunts of effort. A deep roar from 張飛 at 0s and
from 呂布 at 16s. Sound drops to a hollow low rumble during the slow motion,
then slams back. No dialogue.
~~~~

</details>

## 86. Illustrated Creature Flood Escape

<a href="https://media.beatapi.io/prompt-gallery/seedance-2-5/illustrated-creature-flood-escape-172377/video-1e799988479e3f91.webm">
  <img src="https://media.beatapi.io/prompt-gallery/seedance-2-5/illustrated-creature-flood-escape-172377/poster-ed6b60e54ff6e89e.jpg" alt="Illustrated Creature Flood Escape video preview" width="700" />
</a>

<details>
<summary><strong>Prompt</strong> — Lock the original illustration completely. Preserve the exact art style, brushwork, textures, material rendering, color palette, creature proportions, facial expressions,...</summary>

~~~~text
Lock the original illustration completely. Preserve the exact art style, brushwork, textures, material rendering, color palette, creature proportions, facial expressions, lighting, composition, and every character design exactly as shown. Never change the visual style. Every creature must remain identical in every shot. No text, watermark, logo, or signature. One continuous story. Motion always progresses forward. No creature moves backward. Use cinematic pacing with hard cuts, speed ramps, bullet time, dynamic tracking, volumetric water, realistic physics, motion blur, subtle film grain, dramatic lighting, lens droplets, cinematic depth of field, bold camera language, emotional facial animation, and seamless transitions.

0-3s

Without warning, the peaceful group suddenly notices every bird exploding into the sky. Tiny ripples appear beneath their feet. A deep rumble grows louder. Extreme close-up of frightened eyes. Fast push-in toward the rabbit. Hard cut to a gigantic wall of water rising behind them. Lightning-fast whip pan reveals the impossible tsunami racing toward the camera. Deep bass hit.

3-6s

The wave crashes over everyone. Water engulfs the frame. Camera tumbles underwater with bubbles, debris, flowers, leaves and sunlight cutting through the current. Every creature is separated naturally by the force. Slow-motion for one second before time accelerates again.

6-10s

Rapid cross-cut survival sequence. The rabbit paddles frantically with long ears floating behind. The lion powers through the waves with strong strokes. The rhino kicks heavily while struggling to stay above water. The giant blue triceratops swims like a massive whale. Frogs leap from floating debris. Tiny creatures cling to drifting branches. Cats desperately paddle while trying not to sink. Dynamic handheld water-level camera alternating with overhead drone shots.

10-15s

The current becomes more violent. Floating logs collide. The turtle spins helplessly in a whirlpool before escaping. The sheep nearly slips beneath the surface until the lion nudges it upward. The black cat loses its grip and disappears beneath a wave. Silence for half a second except muffled underwater sound.

15-20s

The gray kitten dives underwater searching. Cinematic underwater tracking follows the kitten weaving between drifting plants and bubbles. A frightened turtle struggles below. The kitten reaches it, grabs its shell with tiny paws and guides it upward toward sunlight breaking through the surface.

20-25s

Both burst above the water. The exhausted kitten climbs onto the turtle's shell. The turtle begins swimming steadily through the endless flood while the kitten balances carefully, soaked but relieved. Around them every animal continues fighting the current in its own unique way. Long aerial tracking shot follows their path.

25-28s

The current slowly weakens. One by one the surviving animals regroup behind the turtle. The rabbit swims beside it. Frogs hop onto floating wood. The lion keeps everyone together. The enormous blue triceratops shields the smaller creatures from remaining waves. Warm sunlight finally pierces the storm clouds.

28-30s

A majestic vertical drone shot rises high into the sky. The flood forms beautiful winding rivers across the landscape. At the center, the tiny kitten sits safely on the turtle's shell leading the group forward while every surviving creature follows behind. The music swells into an emotional orchestral finale. Hold the final frame like an animated movie poster as golden light reflects across the water.
~~~~

</details>

## 87. Thousand-Year Static Landscape

  <img src="https://media.beatapi.io/prompt-gallery/seedance-2-5/thousand-year-static-landscape/poster-906f12352c98197d.jpg" alt="Thousand-Year Static Landscape video preview" width="700" />
</a>

<details>
<summary><strong>Prompt</strong> — A 30 second film from ONE completely locked, static camera position that never moves, never pans, never zooms, never cuts. The same view of the same patch of ground for one...</summary>

~~~~text
A 30 second film from ONE completely locked, static camera position that never moves, never pans, never zooms, never cuts. The same view of the same patch of ground for one thousand years, time passing at enormous speed. In the foreground of every single frame sits a large mossy grey boulder. It never moves. It is present and recognisable in every frame of the film from first to last. [0:00-0:03] Ancient temperate forest. Huge old trees, deep green undergrowth, mist between the trunks, soft grey daylight. [0:03-0:06] Trees fall and are dragged away. A clearing opens. Stumps, churned mud, a first thin column of smoke. [0:06-0:09] A single timber and thatch hut rises beside the boulder. A small vegetable plot. Chickens. [0:09-0:12] A village grows around it. More huts, fences, a dirt track, wood smoke, sheep in the field beyond. [0:12-0:15] A stone wall is built, incorporating the boulder into its base. A church tower rises behind. Snow falls and melts. Seasons flicker past. [0:15-0:18] Fire. The village burns, timbers collapse, smoke blackens the sky. The wall and the boulder survive. [0:18-0:21] Rebuilding in brick. A cobbled road. A chimney, then many chimneys. Industrial smoke, grey air, rain. [0:21-0:24] The town becomes a city. Concrete rises. The old wall is demolished. The boulder sits in a small paved square, surrounded by buildings. [0:24-0:26] Glass towers. Traffic light streaking past at night. The boulder is fenced off, a small green plaque beside it. [0:26-0:28] Abandonment. Windows dark, glass cracked, weeds through the paving, ivy climbing the towers. Silence. [0:28-0:30] The towers have collapsed and gone. Young forest has grown over everything. Mist between the trunks. The frame is now identical to the opening frame, the same boulder in the same position under the same soft grey light. Camera: absolutely locked and static for all 30 seconds. No movement of any kind. No cuts, no dissolves, no transitions — one continuous transforming shot. Style: photoreal, cinematic, natural overcast light throughout, muted natural colour, seasons and weather flickering rapidly past. Audio: diegetic only, evolving with the era — forest birds, axes, village life, church bell, fire, industrial machinery, city traffic, then silence, then birds again. NO MUSIC, no score, no soundtrack, no instruments. No text, no captions, no titles, no watermarks, no camera movement, no cuts.
~~~~

## 88. Lunar Cafe Dialogue

<a href="https://media.beatapi.io/prompt-gallery/seedance-2-5/lunar-cafe-dialogue/video-5ce98162dc4cdfe2.webm">
  <img src="https://media.beatapi.io/prompt-gallery/seedance-2-5/lunar-cafe-dialogue/poster-e5e89e003fd4382b.jpg" alt="Lunar Cafe Dialogue video preview" width="700" />
</a>

<details>
<summary><strong>Prompt</strong> — META STYLE Black-and-white 35mm arthouse cinema, 1960s European café-film look. High-contrast monochrome, soft film grain, gentle bloom on the lamps, shallow depth of field on...</summary>

~~~~text
META

STYLE
Black-and-white 35mm arthouse cinema, 1960s European café-film look. High-contrast monochrome, soft film grain, gentle bloom on the lamps, shallow depth of field on close-ups.

LIGHT
Night interior lit only by practicals: hanging globe lamps, the espresso machine's glow, and cold light through the window from the lunar plain. Soft key from the window side, deep shadow in the corners, clear catch-lights in the eyes.

COLOR
None. Full grayscale, rich mid-grays on skin, no tint of any kind.

TEXTURE
Fine grain. Chrome holds soft rolling reflections, glass refracts the lamps in small bright points, skin keeps visible pore-level texture with no smoothing.

MOOD
Quiet, intimate, unhurried — a conversation overheard in a place that is ordinary to the people in it.

SCENE CONTEXT
A near-empty late-night café on the surface of the moon. A man and a woman sit across a small round table beside the wide window, talking about the water. Neither of them ever looks outside.

CHARACTERS

The woman: early thirties, dark bob haircut, intelligent face, dark turtleneck with the cuffs visible at her wrists, thin earrings, light coat over her chair. She sits camera-left. Identical in every shot.

The man: mid-thirties, dark slightly messy hair, light stubble, shadowed under-eyes, dark jacket over an open-collar light shirt, jacket sleeve visible at his wrists. He sits camera-right. Identical in every shot.

LOCATION MAP
Foreground: their small round table. Her espresso cup and the single tall glass of water sit on her side, close to her right hand. His espresso cup and spoon sit on his side. Nothing else on the table.
Midground: small round tables, worn tiled floor, hanging globe lamps, chrome espresso machine at the counter.
Background: a wide window along the wall beside them. Beyond the glass, a grey lunar plain with low craters, black sky, and Earth small and low near the horizon.

FIRST FRAME / BLOCKING
Interior medium two-shot, 47°, eye level. The woman sits camera-left, the man camera-right, the window and lunar plain behind them, café interior deeper in the room. Her hands are around her cup with the water glass beside it, his forearms rest on the table.

FORMAT MODE
Timed multishot with HARD CUTS only at 6.0s, 8.5s, 11.0s, 13.0s, 16.0s, 19.0s, 22.5s, 25.0s and 28.0s. The camera cuts only at these points.

STORYBOARD

SHOT 1 (0.0–6.0s)
Medium two-shot, 47°, faint 1–2 cm breathing drift.
The woman, eyes on him: "Do you think people get more honest when they're tired?"
The man answers late, eyes coming up off the table: "No. Their lies just get less inventive."

SHOT 2 (6.0–8.5s)
Extreme close-up, 12°, of the woman's hands on her side of the table, dark turtleneck cuffs at her wrists. Her fingers leave her cup, lift the tall glass of water, and set it back down in the same place, the water rocking once.
The woman, speaking over the image: "It's different tonight."

SHOT 3 (8.5–11.0s)
Close portrait of the man, 18°, eyes down.
The man: "They changed the water."

SHOT 4 (11.0–13.0s)
Extreme close-up, 12°, of the man's hand on his side of the table, dark jacket sleeve at his wrist, turning his spoon a half-turn on the saucer.
The woman, speaking over the image from off-frame: "Again?"

SHOT 5 (13.0–16.0s)
Medium close-up of the man, 29°, lunar plain soft behind his shoulder, eyes still on his cup.
The man: "The old one remembered too much."

SHOT 6 (16.0–19.0s)
Close-up of the woman, 18°. Her eyes come up and hold his.
The woman: "Water can't remember people."

SHOT 7 (19.0–22.5s)
Medium close-up of the man, 29°. He swallows once, then looks up at her.
The man: "Probably not. But people can't remember the past either."

SHOT 8 (22.5–25.0s)
Medium two-shot, 47°, lunar plain behind them. She leans in slightly.
The woman: "Then what do we remember?"

SHOT 9 (25.0–28.0s)
Close portrait of the man, 18°. One breath, then his eyes come up and hold hers.
The man: "The last version we could live with."

SHOT 10 (28.0–30.0s)
Close-up of the woman, 18°, the lunar plain and black sky soft behind her through the glass. No dialogue. Her face stills, one slow blink, her eyes drop toward her cup and then drift slightly aside. The shot holds on her to the last frame, the image staying full and lit.

PERFORMANCE
Both faces stay mostly still, so small movements read. Emotion comes through eyelids, brow corners, jaw and breath rather than expression. Blinks work as punctuation — withheld through the important lines, released after. She runs from light and half-joking, to quieter, to genuinely curious, to quiet recognition at the end. He runs from dry amusement, to flat matter-of-fact, to unguarded tiredness on the last line. Living eyes with catch-lights, natural skin, precise lip sync whenever a speaking face is in frame. Every line is delivered unhurried and complete inside its own shot.

PHYSICS
Steam curls off the cups and the espresso machine. Water rocks and settles in the glass. The cup meets the saucer with weight, the spoon rings faintly on chrome. Nothing beyond the window moves.

AUDIO
The low hum of an enclosed room, a faint air-system whisper, the hiss of the espresso machine, a spoon against a saucer, a swallow of water. No music anywhere. Voices sit close and dry, as in a small room late at night.

OUTPUT SETTINGS
Black and white throughout, real-time, no slow motion.

POSITIVE LOCKS
The image stays fully monochrome in every shot.
The woman speaks her five lines and the man speaks his five lines, exactly as assigned in the storyboard, and neither ever speaks the other's words.
The single glass of water stays on the woman's side of the table for the whole scene, and only she touches it.
The woman stays camera-left and the man camera-right in every two-shot.
Hands in the extreme close-ups are identified by sleeve — turtleneck cuff for her, jacket sleeve for him.
The table holds only two espresso cups on saucers, two spoons and one glass of water.
The interior reads clearly as a café — table, cups, lamps, counter, espresso machine.
The window shows a grey lunar plain, black sky and small distant Earth wherever it is visible, and nothing beyond the glass ever moves.
Both characters keep their exact appearance and seats across every shot.
All ten lines are spoken in full and finish inside their own shot.
Cuts land only at the stated seconds.
The video ends on the held close-up of the woman's face, the image full and lit to the final frame.
~~~~

</details>
